Re-adding the mapping is forbidden. Worth flagging for the human reviewer: the manifest carries no `droppedSemantics` block for `Drop.createdByUser`, so this loss is undeclared — the manifest describes only cascade/orphanRemoval losses, and `@CreatedBy` auditing propagation is a third category it does not model.\n\nOne design note for review: `getFinishedPollableTaskIds()` is the literal Shape-B translation of the original `finishedDate is not null` predicate, but `pollable_task` grows with every async operation, so materialising every finished id on each `GET /api/drops?imported=…` is a real cardinality risk in production. The semantically identical inverse — fetching the *unfinished* ids and flipping the predicate — would be far smaller in practice, at the cost of a double negation. The operator's fail-fast command stops the reactor at `webapp` (`1214 tests, 1 failure`); I also ran the full reactor with `-Dmaven.test.failure.ignore=true` to confirm `common` (254), `test-common` (7) and `cli` (538) are all clean, i.e. nothing after `webapp` hides further query breakage.\n\n## Not fixed — out of scope\n\n**`DropServiceTest.testCreateDrop:104` — `assertNotNull(createDrop.getCreatedByUser())`**\n\nNot a broken query: no JPQL/Criteria path or join is involved, and the failure signature is a value unexpectedly null after a flush. `Drop.createdByUser` carries Spring Data's `@CreatedBy`; the refactoring made it `@Transient`, and `AuditingEntityListener` resolves `@CreatedBy` through the JPA-metamodel-backed mapping context, which no longer contains that property. So the auditor is never written to the field and `created_by_user_id` stays NULL on insert.\n\nConfirmed by controlled comparison rather than inference: `TMServiceTest:237`, `TMTextUnitHistoryServiceTest` (6 assertions) and `UserServiceTest:71` make the same `getCreatedByUser()` non-null assertion and all pass — those entities still have `@CreatedBy` on a mapped `@ManyToOne`. Auditing works generally; only the unmapped field is skipped. `TranslationKit.createdByUser` has the same exposure but no test asserts it.\n\nRepairing it would mean writing the auditor explicitly in `DropService.createDrop` or in `Drop`'s lifecycle callback — application/domain logic outside the permitted edit surface (query code plus the generated services), and exactly the \"developer decision about the new module boundary\" the task says to report rather than fix. Re-adding the mapping is forbidden. Worth flagging for the human reviewer: the manifest carries no `droppedSemantics` block for `Drop.createdByUser`, so this loss is undeclared — the manifest describes only cascade/orphanRemoval losses, and `@CreatedBy` auditing propagation is a third category it does not model.\n\nOne design note for review: `getFinishedPollableTaskIds()` is the literal Shape-B translation of the original `finishedDate is not null` predicate, but `pollable_task` grows with every async operation, so materialising every finished id on each `GET /api/drops?imported=…` is a real cardinality risk in production. The semantically identical inverse — fetching the *unfinished* ids and flipping the predicate — would be far smaller in practice, at the cost of a double negation. I kept the direct translation for reviewability; the swap is a one-line change in `PollableTaskServiceImpl` plus flipping the two predicates in `DropSpecification`.\n\nAll edits are uncommitted, as required.","ttft_ms":2781,"ttft_stream_ms":2263,"time_to_request_ms":93,"type":"result","duration_ms":2415525,"uuid":"cd0bf896-3b4c-420a-81ee-9b4d72001bfc"}
